/* Diabetes HFpEF GLP1 Study */

/* Run the below command to select the required database */
use ORD_Sundaram_202108013D
go

/* loop diuretics */
select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.DischargeDateTime, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, b.FillDateTime, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty

into #tempmedloop

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%furosemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%torsemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumetanide%' or
		LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Ethacrynic acid%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lasix%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumex%' 
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.DischargeDateTime, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime,b.FillDateTime, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%furosemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%torsemide%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumetanide%' or
		LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Ethacrynic acid%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lasix%' or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%bumex%' 
	
/* GLP1 Analogue */

select distinct LocalDrugNameWithDose from CDWWork.Dim.LocalDrug where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select top 100* from Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, b.FillDateTime as GLP1FillTime , b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply

into Dflt.tempglp1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_Loop_BNP_PBNP as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, b.FillDateTime as GLP1FillTime , b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_Loop_BNP_PBNP as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.tempglp1 --13,515

select scrssn, min(GLP1FillTime) as GLP1FillTime
into #tempfilltime
from Dflt.tempglp1 group by scrssn

/*select *

into #tempGLP1DateFilter

from #tempfilltime where GLP1FillTime between '2017-01-01 00:00:00' and '2021-12-31 23:59:59'*/



select scrssn, sum(FillNumber) as FillNumber 
into #tempfillnumber
from Dflt.tempglp1 group by scrssn 


select distinct b.scrssn, b.GLP1FillTime, a.FillNumber
into #tempfilldate
from #tempfillnumber as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempfilltime as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn 

select distinct scrssn from #tempfilldate --13,515

select distinct b.scrssn, b.GLP1FillTime, b.FillNumber,a.patientsid, a.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime
into #date
from Dflt.tempglp1 as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempfilldate as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and b.GLP1FillTime=a.glp1filltime
where b.GLP1FillTime between '2015-01-01 00:00:00' and '2021-12-31 23:59:59'
and b.GLP1FillTime >= dateadd(day, 30, a.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime)

select distinct scrssn from #date --6703

select * from #date
drop table #Date
/* Remove patients that were on DPP4 1 year prior to GLP1 initiation */
select distinct a.scrssn, a.GLP1FillTime, a.PatientSID, b.FillDateTime

into #DPP4

from #date as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
      LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%' 
--and b.FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
union all

select distinct a.scrssn, a.GLP1FillTime, a.PatientSID, b.FillDateTime
from #date as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
      LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%' 

select distinct *

into #DPP4Date

from #DPP4
where FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from #DPP4 --5,395
select distinct scrssn from #DPP4Date --2,278

select distinct a.*--, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10ProcedureCode

into #GLP1excludeDpp4

from #date as a where a.fillnumber >=2
AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #dpp4date as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)

select distinct scrssn from #GLP1excludeDpp4--4,041

/* Extracting patients with 1 or more refill */

select distinct a.*--, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10ProcedureCode

into #GLP1excludeDpp4

from #date as a where a.fillnumber >=1
AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #dpp4date as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)

select distinct scrssn from #GLP1excludeDpp4--4,163

/*@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@*/

select distinct b.*

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1

from  #GLP1excludeDpp4 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#date as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn 
where a.fillnumber >=2 --and b.GLP1FillTime between '2017-01-01 00:00:00' and '2021-12-31 23:59:59'

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1 --4,041

/* Patients on Loop diuretics with more than 1 refill 1 year preceding to the start of GLP1 */
select distinct a.*, b.FillNumber as loopFillNumber, b.FillDateTime 

into #temploop

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempmedloop as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
and b.Fillnumber >1

select distinct scrssn from #temploop

drop table #temploop

/*Patients on elevated BNP/PBNP 1 year preceding to the start of GLP1 */

select distinct a.*, b.LabChemCompleteDateTime, b.LabChemResultNumericValue
into #tempBNPPBNP

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_BNP_PBNP as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from #tempBNPPBNP

/*Patients with HF ICD codes 1 year preceding to the start of GLP1 */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, b.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime--, b.comorbidity_ICD9Code as HFICDCode

into #tempHF

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from #tempHF

select distinct scrssn 

into #tempuniqueglp1
from #temploop
union all

select distinct scrssn
from #tempBNPPBNP
union all

select distinct scrssn
from #tempHF

select distinct scrssn from #tempuniqueglp1

/* combine all 3 to get the final cohort */
drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final

select distinct a.*--, b.FillNumber, b.FillDateTime

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempmedloop as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
and b.Fillnumber >1
union all

select distinct a.*--, b.LabChemCompleteDateTime, b.LabChemResultNumericValue, b.LabChemTestName 

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_BNP_PBNP as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
union all

select distinct a.*--, b.DischargeDateTime, b.comorbidity_ICD9Code as HFICDCode

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final --2,663

select * from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final

/* HFiEF */
drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HFiEF_GLP1

select distinct a.*, b.Low_Value, b.ValueDateTime

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFiEF_GLP1final

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final as a with (NOLOCK) inner join 
Src.VINCI_TIU_NLP_LVEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where b.Low_Value between 10 and 49 and b.ValueDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and a.glp1filltime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFiEF_GLP1final --1,416


/*select distinct a.*, b.Low_Value, b.ValueDateTime

into Dflt.Diabetes_HFiEF_GLP1final2

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final as a with (NOLOCK) inner join 
Src.VINCI_TIU_NLP_LVEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where b.Low_Value between 10 and 40 and b.ValueDateTime < a.glp1filltime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HFiEF_GLP1final2 --2,073 (EF 10-49 any time before GLP fill time); 1,876 (EF 10-40 any time before GLP fill time)
drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HFiEF_GLP1final2 */

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final as a where a.Fillnumber >=2
AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #exclusioncodesGLP1 as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)
--AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #exclusioncodesSGLT2 as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11 --2,385

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11

/* Exclude patients with EF less than 50 */
select distinct a.*, b.Low_Value, b.ValueDateTime

into #templess50

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join 
Src.VINCI_TIU_NLP_LVEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where b.Low_Value between 10 and 49 and b.ValueDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and a.glp1filltime

select distinct scrssn from #templess50 where low_value < 10--1,243

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final1less50

select distinct a.*--, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10ProcedureCode

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final1less50

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11 as a where a.fillnumber >=2
AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #templess50 as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final1less50--1,142

/* Add patientsid from cohortcrosswalk for each scrssn */

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions

select distinct a.scrssn, a.glp1filltime, a.fillnumber, b.patientsid
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final1less50 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.CohortCrosswalk as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions--1,142

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions where fillnumber=2

select *
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final1
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions

/* DPP4 Control cohort */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, b.FillDateTime as DPP4FillTime, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty, b.DrugNameWithoutDose

into #tempDPP4

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_Loop_BNP_PBNP as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%'

union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.FirstHF_dischargedatetime, b.FillDateTime as DPP4FillTime, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty, b.DrugNameWithoutDose
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_Loop_BNP_PBNP as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
 LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%'


select distinct scrssn from #tempDPP4 --111,303

/* Find the DPP4 Fill time */

select scrssn, min(DPP4FillTime) as DPP4FillTime
into #temp1
from #tempDpp4 group by scrssn

select distinct scrssn from #temp1 -- 111,303

/* Add the refills for DPP4 */

select scrssn, sum(FillNumber) as FillNumber 
into #tempfillnumberDPP4
from #tempDPP4 group by scrssn 

select distinct scrssn from #tempfillnumberDPP4 --111,303
 
select distinct  b.scrssn, a.DPP4FillTime, b.FillNumber
into #temp2
from #temp1 as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempfillnumberDPP4 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn --and a.DPP4FillTime= b.DPP4FillTime

select distinct scrssn from #temp2 --111,303

select distinct b.scrssn, b.DPP4FillTime, b.FillNumber,a.patientsid, a.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime
into #date1
from #tempdpp4 as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#temp2 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and b.DPP4FillTime=a.DPP4FillTime
where b.DPP4FillTime between '2015-01-01 00:00:00' and '2021-12-31 23:59:59'
and b.DPP4FillTime >= dateadd(day, 30, a.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime)

select distinct scrssn from #date1--4391


/* Remove GLP1 from DPP4 */

select distinct a.scrssn, a.DPP4FillTime, a.PatientSID, b.FillDateTime

into #GLP1

from #date1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'
union all

select distinct a.scrssn, a.DPP4FillTime, a.PatientSID, b.FillDateTime
from #date1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select distinct scrssn from #GLP1 --743

select * 
into #GLP11
from #GLP1 where FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, DPP4FillTime) 

select distinct scrssn from #glp11 --100

into #Dpp4excludeGLP1

from #date1 as a where a.fillnumber >=2
AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #glp11 as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)

select distinct scrssn from #Dpp4excludeGLP1--3966

where b.FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.DPP4FillTime)
and b.Fillnumber >1
union all

from #Dpp4excludeGLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_BNP_PBNP as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.DPP4FillTime)
union all

from #Dpp4excludeGLP1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime between dateadd(month, -12, a.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.DPP4FillTime)

/* GLP1 */
/*BMI*/

/* Select height and weight values from vital signs view to calculate bmi */
drop table #tempDiabetesVitals
select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into #tempDiabetesVitals

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.GLP1FillTime)) AND
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND 
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.SGLT2FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.SGLT2FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime <= b.GLP1FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.SGLT2FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.SGLT2FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime <= b.GLP1FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')

select distinct scrssn from #tempDiabetesVitals

/* Seperate weight records into a table */
select * 

into #tempweight

from #tempDiabetesVitals where vitaltype = 'WEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempweight

select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempweight1
from #tempweight group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempweightfinal
from #tempweight as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempweight1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightfinal --1,117

/* Seperate height records into a table */
select *  

into #tempheight

from #tempDiabetesVitals where vitaltype = 'HEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempheight

select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempheight1
from #tempheight group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempheightfinal
from #tempheight as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempheight1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightfinal --1,143

/* Combine data from table weight and height to obtain the bmi for individual patient */
drop table  Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI

select distinct a.patientsid, a.ScrSSN, cast(a.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as HeightTime, a.VitalResultNumeric as heightresult, 
cast(b.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as WeightTime,b.VitalResultNumeric as weightresult, ((b.VitalResultNumeric*703)/(a.VitalResultNumeric*a.VitalResultNumeric)) as bmi

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI

from #tempheightfinal as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempweightfinal as b with (NOLOCK) on a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN
--where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime=b.VitalSignTakenDateTime --a.SGLT2FillTime=b.SGLT2FillTime and

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI where bmi < 30 --114

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI where bmi between 30 and 35 --238

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI where bmi between 35 and 40 --306

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI where bmi > 40 --458

/* Baseline Measurements for GLP1 Cohort */
/* Demographics */
/* Age */
drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Age

select a.ScrSSN, a.glp1Filltime, b.DOB,b.SEX
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_DOB
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.VitalStatus_Mini as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn

select *, datediff( year, DOB, glp1Filltime) as age
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Age
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_DOB

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Age where SEX ='F'

/* Date of Death */

select a.ScrSSN,  b.DOD
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_DOD
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.VitalStatus_Master as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn
where b.DOD is not null

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_DOD--352

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_DOD

select a.Scrssn, b.MPI_DOD

into #tempDOD

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.SDAF.DAFMaster as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.MPI_scrssn
where b.MPI_DOD is not null  

/* Race GLP1 */

Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn

select distinct scrssn from #temprace

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Race
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.PatSub_PatientRace as b with (NOLOCK) on a.patientsid= b.patientsid

into #temp1
from #temprace1

--into #temp2

from #temprace1

/* CAD- PCI & CABG */

select a.*, b.SURGDATE

into #tempCABGGLP1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
src.VASQIP_nso_cardiac as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn
where b.cpt01 in ('33533','33534','33535','33536','33542','33545','33548')
and b.SURGDATE <= a.GLP1filltime /* Check the dates */

select * from #tempCABGGLP1

select a.*, b.ProcedureDateTime

into #tempPCIGLP1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.CART_PCI as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where b.ProcedureDateTime <= a.GLP1filltime /* Check the dates */

Select distinct patientsid, scrssn, GLP1FillTime, surgdate

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_cadCabgpci

from #tempCABGGLP1
union all

select distinct patientsid, scrssn, GLP1FillTime, ProcedureDateTime
from #tempPCIGLP1
union all

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_CAD

	c.LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'

union all

	c.LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'
and a.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime)

/* Hematocrit */
/* Check for the labchemtestname in the dimension table */
select distinct labchemtestname from CDWWork.Dim.Labchemtest 
WHERE (LabChemTestName like '%hematocrit%' OR 
	LabChemTestName like '%hem%crit%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%packed%cell%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%PCV%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%VPRC%' OR 
	LabChemTestName like '%EVF%' OR
	LabChemTestName like '%erythrocy%vol%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%haemto%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%hct%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%crit%' or
	LabChemTestName like '%h%&%h%')
	AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%serotype%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%PCV [0-9]%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%HPCV%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%cryocrit%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%dna%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%hiv%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%critical%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%A.%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%homocys%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%alpha%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%alcohol%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%amphetamine%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%amino%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%phago%' and
	LabChemTestName not like '%gonorr%' and

/* ALT (Alanine Aminotransferase) */
/* Check for the labchemtestname in the dimension table */
select distinct labchemtestname from CDWWork.Dim.Labchemtest 
WHERE
(LabChemTestName like '%ALT%' OR 
LabChemTestName like '%alanine%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%aminotransferase%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%SGPT%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%SGOT%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%transaminase%' OR
LabChemTestName like '%GPT%' ) 
AND LabChemTestName not like '%ALTER%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%HEALTH%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Hepatitis%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Naltrex%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Kilodalton%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Malt%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Specialty%'
AND LabChemTestName not like '%Salt%'

(LabChemTestName like '%ALT%' OR

LabChemTestName like '%aminotransferase%' OR

/* Blood Pressure */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric, a.systolic, a.diastolic,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BP

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.GLP1FillTime)) AND
c.VitalType in ('%Blood pressure%')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,a.systolic, a.diastolic,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND 
c.VitalType like ('%Blood pressure%')

b.FillNumber > '1'

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.GLP1FillTime, b.FillDateTime, b.LocalDrugNameWithDose, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where b.FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, a.GLP1FillTime) and a.GLP1FillTime and 
b.FillNumber > '1'

select top 1000* from #tempMedications

/*Aspirin */

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Aspirin 

from #tempMedications where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Aspirin%'

/*Statin */
/* Check for the LocalDrugNameWithDose in the dimension table */

select * from CDWWork.Dim.LocalDrug where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%atorvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lovastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%pravastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%rosuvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%simvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Fluvastatin%'

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.GLP1FillTime, b.FillDateTime, b.LocalDrugNameWithDose, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty

into #tempstatin

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%atorvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lovastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%pravastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%rosuvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%simvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Fluvastatin%'
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.ScrSSN, a.GLP1FillTime, b.FillDateTime, b.LocalDrugNameWithDose, b.FillNumber, b.DaysSupply, b.Qty

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%atorvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lovastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%pravastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%rosuvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%simvastatin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Fluvastatin%'

select *

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Statin

from #tempstatin 
where FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(month, 12, GLP1FillTime) 
and FillNumber > '1'

//** After the drug initiation - Extraction **//

/* All HF hospitalization after GLP1 initiation */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime,c.ICD9Code,b.AdmitDiagnosis
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.GLP1_HFHospitalization

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime
union all

/*ICD10 Codes*/
select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10Code, b.AdmitDiagnosis

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')
and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.GLP1_HFHospitalization--177

/* Use only Discharge diagnosis table to identify HF hospitalization events - GLP1 */

select distinct a.*, b.AdmitDateTime

into Dflt.GLP1_HFDDHospi

/*ICD9 Code */

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatDischargeDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')
					and b.AdmitDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime
union all
/*ICD10 Code */

select distinct a.*, b.AdmitDateTime

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatDischargeDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')
and b.AdmitDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.GLP1_HFDDHospi--455

select distinct a.*,b.FillDateTime, b.FillNumber as GLP1FillNumber

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%empagliflozin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dapagliflozin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%canagliflozin%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%ertugliflozin%' 
--and b.FillDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime

select distinct * 
into Dflt.GLP1SGLT2
from #GLP1SGLT2 where GLP1FillNumber >=3
and FillDateTime >= GLP1FillTime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.GLP1SGLT2 --219

drop table Dflt.GLP1SGLT2

/*BMI*/
/* Select height and weight values from vital signs view to calculate bmi */
drop table #tempDiabetesVitals
select top 1000* from Src.Vital_VitalSign

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into #tempDiabetesVitalsGLP1

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(month, 3, b.GLP1FillTime) AND
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(month, 3, b.GLP1FillTime) AND 
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.GLP1FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime >= b.GLP1FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.GLP1FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.GLP1FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime >= b.GLP1FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')

select distinct scrssn from #tempDiabetesVitalsGLP1--1105

/* Seperate weight records into a table */
select * 

into #tempweightGLP1After

from #tempDiabetesVitalsGLP1 where vitaltype = 'WEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightGLP1After 

/*select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempweight1GLP1After
from #tempweightGLP1After group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempweightfinalGLP1After
from #tempweightGLP1After as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempweight1GLP1After as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightfinalGLP1After*/

/* Seperate height records into a table */
select *  

into #tempheightGLP1After

from #tempDiabetesVitalsGLP1 where vitaltype = 'HEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightGLP1After

/*select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempheight1GLP1After
from #tempheightGLP1After group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempheightfinalGLP1After
from #tempheightGLP1After as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempheight1GLP1After as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightfinalGLP1After*/

/* Combine data from table weight and height to obtain the bmi for individual patient */

select distinct a.patientsid, a.ScrSSN, cast(a.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as HeightTime, a.VitalResultNumeric as heightresult, 
cast(b.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as WeightTime,b.VitalResultNumeric as weightresult, ((b.VitalResultNumeric*703)/(a.VitalResultNumeric*a.VitalResultNumeric)) as bmi

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI_After

from #tempheightGLP1After as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempweightGLP1After as b with (NOLOCK) on a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime=b.VitalSignTakenDateTime --a.GLP1FillTime=b.GLP1FillTime and

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI_After where bmi between 30 and 35 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI_After where bmi between 35 and 40 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI_After where bmi > 40

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_BMI_After where bmi < 30

where LabChemCompleteDateTime > GLP1FillTime

					and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.GLP1FillTime
union all

/* Baseline Measurements for DPP4GLP1 Cohort */
/* Demographics */
/* Age */

select a.ScrSSN, a.DPP4Filltime, b.DOB,b.SEX
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_DOB
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.VitalStatus_Mini as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn

select *, datediff( year, DOB, DPP4Filltime) as age
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Age
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_DOB

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Age where SEX ='F'

/* Date of Death */

select a.ScrSSN,  b.DOD
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_DOD
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.VitalStatus_Mini as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn
where b.DOD is not null

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_DOD --433

Src.PatSub_PatientRace as b with (NOLOCK) on a.patientsid= b.patientsid

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Race

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Race

from #temprace
union all

select  *
from #temprace1

/* Hypertension */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime as comorbidity_Dischargedatetime, c.ICD9Code as comorbidity_ICD9Code
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Hyper

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join

					'C78.4', 'C78.5','C78.6', 'C78.7', 'C78.89','C79.9','C79.11','C79.2','C79.31','C79.32','C79.49',
					'C79.51','C79.52','C79.60','C79.70','C79.81','C79.82','C79.89','C80.0','C80.1')

/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Malignancy

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('196.0','196.1','196.2','196.3','196.5','196.6','196.8','196.9','197.0','197.1','197.2','197.3','197.4','197.5',
					'197.6','197.7','197.8','198.0','198.1','198.2','198.3','198.4','198.5','198.6','198.7','198.81','198.82','198.89','199.0','199.1')

/*BMI*/
/* Select height and weight values from vital signs view to calculate bmi */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.DPP4FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into #tempDiabetesVitals

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.DPP4FillTime)) AND
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.DPP4FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4FillTime))AND 
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.DPP4FillTime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime <= b.DPP4FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.DPP4FillTime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4FillTime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime <= b.DPP4FillTime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')

select distinct scrssn from #tempDiabetesVitals --1026

drop table #tempweight1
/* Seperate weight records into a table */
select * 

into #tempweight

from #tempDiabetesVitals where vitaltype = 'WEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempweight

select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempweight1
from #tempweight group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempweightfinal
from #tempweight as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempweight1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightfinal --1006

drop table #tempheight1
/* Seperate height records into a table */
select *  

into #tempheight

from #tempDiabetesVitals where vitaltype = 'HEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempheight

select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempheight1
from #tempheight group by scrssn

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempheightfinal
from #tempheight as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempheight1 as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightfinal--1027

/* Combine data from table weight and height to obtain the bmi for individual patient */
drop table  Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI

select distinct a.patientsid, a.ScrSSN, cast(a.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as HeightTime, a.VitalResultNumeric as heightresult, 
cast(b.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as WeightTime,b.VitalResultNumeric as weightresult, ((b.VitalResultNumeric*703)/(a.VitalResultNumeric*a.VitalResultNumeric)) as bmi

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI

from #tempheightfinal as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempweightfinal as b with (NOLOCK) on a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN --1005
--where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime=b.VitalSignTakenDateTime --a.DPP4FillTime=b.DPP4FillTime and

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi between 30 and 35 --272

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi between 35 and 40 --236

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi > 40 --270

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI where bmi < 30 --227

	LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'

where c.LabChemTestName like '%A1C%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'
and a.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime)                                
union all

from Src.Chem_LabChem_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join

where c.LabChemTestName like '%A1C%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'
and a.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime)  
union all

select b.*, a.LabChemResultValue, a.LabChemResultNumericValue, a.LabChemCompleteDateTime, c.LabChemTestName

from Src.Chem_PatientLabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID

	c.LabChemTestName not like '%ZZ%' AND
	c.LabChemTestName not like '%XX%'
and a.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, DPP4Filltime)

/* Hemoglobin */

select distinct labchemtestname from CDWWork.Dim.Labchemtest 
WHERE 
   (labchemtestname like '%hemoglob%' or 
    labchemtestname like '%HB%' or 
	LabChemTestName like '%HGB%') and
	labchemtestname not like 'z%' AND
	labchemtestname not like 'x%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%' AND 
	LabChemTestName not like '%HBV%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%Anti-HB%' AND 
	LabChemTestName not like '%Hepatitis%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%ELECTROPHORESIS%' AND
	LabChemTestName not like '%HBSAG%' AND

	LabChemTestName NOT LIKE '%A1C%'
	AND LabChemTestName NOT LIKE '%CELL%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%RATE%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%/100wbc%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%sperm%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%HBV%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hbc%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hiv%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%anti%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hep%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%butyl%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%VITROS%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hormone%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%hbs%'

		LabChemTestName like '%urea%nit%' OR
		LabChemTestName like '%blood%urea%nitro%' ) AND
		LabChemTestName not like '%Pylori%' AND
		LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%'

CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE (LabChemTestName like '%[^a-z]bun[^a-z]%' OR 
		LabChemTestName like '%urea%nit%' OR
		LabChemTestName like '%blood%urea%nitro%' ) AND
		LabChemTestName not like '%Pylori%' AND
		LabChemTestName not like '%ratio%'

	AND LabChemTestName not like '%gote%'

from Src.Chem_PatientLabChem as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.LabChemTest as c with (NOLOCK) on a.LabChemTestSID=c.LabChemTestSID
WHERE
(LabChemTestName like '%[^a-z]AST%' or
  LabChemTestName like '%GOT%' or
  LabChemTestName like '%transaminase%' or
  LabChemTestName like '%aspartate%' or
  LabChemTestName like  '%aminotransferase%') 
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%astra%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%ergotamine%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%dermatophag%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%escargot%' 
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%oligotyping%'
	AND LabChemTestName not like '%gote%'

select *
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_AST
from #tempAST
where LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -6, DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, DPP4Filltime)

/* Blood Pressure */

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric, a.diastolic, a.Systolic,
 b.DPP4Filltime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BP

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(DAY, 1, b.DPP4Filltime)) AND
c.VitalType in ('%Blood pressure%')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,a.diastolic, a.Systolic,
b.DPP4Filltime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime))AND 
c.VitalType like ('%Blood pressure%')

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BP  --343

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join

CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')
and b.AdmitDateTime between dateadd(year, -10, a.DPP4Filltime) and a.DPP4Filltime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.DPP4GLP1_PriorHFHospitalization--141

/* All HF hospitalization after DPP4GLP1 initiation */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime,c.ICD9Code,b.AdmitDiagnosis
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.DPP4GLP1_HFHospitalization

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('398.91','428.0','428.1','428.20','428.21','428.22','428.23','428.30',
						'428.31','428.32','428.33','428.40','428.41','428.42','428.43','428.9')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

/*ICD10 Codes*/
select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10Code, b.AdmitDiagnosis

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I50.00','I50.1','I50.20','I50.30','I50.40','I50.9',
									'I11.0','I13.0','I13.2','I25.5')
and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.DPP4GLP1_HFHospitalization--145

/*BMI After*/
/* Select height and weight values from vital signs view to calculate bmi */
drop table #tempDiabetesVitals

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.DPP4Filltime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

into #tempDiabetesVitalsDPP4GLP1

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(month, 3, b.DPP4Filltime) AND
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.DPP4Filltime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime > dateadd(month, 3, b.DPP4Filltime) AND 
c.VitalType in ( 'WEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
 b.DPP4Filltime, b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime >= b.DPP4Filltime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')
union all

select distinct a.PatientSID, a.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric,
b.DPP4Filltime,b.ScrSSN, 
c.VitalType

from Src.Vital_VitalSign_Recent as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.VitalType as c with (NOLOCK) on a.VitalTypeSID=c.VitalTypeSID 
--where (a.VitalSignTakenDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, b.DPP4Filltime) and dateadd(day, 1, b.DPP4Filltime))AND
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime >= b.DPP4Filltime and 
c.VitalType in ( 'HEIGHT')

select distinct scrssn from #tempDiabetesVitalsDPP4GLP1--989

into #tempweightDPP4GLP1After

from #tempDiabetesVitalsDPP4GLP1 where vitaltype = 'WEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightDPP4GLP1After--969

from #tempweightDPP4GLP1After group by scrssn

into #tempweightfinalDPP4GLP1After

#tempweight1DPP4GLP1After as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempweightfinalDPP4GLP1After*/

/* Seperate height records into a table */

into #tempheightDPP4GLP1After

from #tempDiabetesVitalsDPP4GLP1 where vitaltype = 'HEIGHT' and VitalResultNumeric != '0'

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightDPP4GLP1After --887

/*select scrssn, max (VitalSignTakenDateTime) as VitalSignTakenDateTime
into #tempheight1DPP4GLP1After

select distinct a.patientsid, b.scrssn, b.VitalSignTakenDateTime, a.VitalResult, a.VitalResultNumeric
into #tempheightfinalDPP4GLP1After

#tempheight1DPP4GLP1After as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and a.VitalSignTakenDateTime= b.VitalSignTakenDateTime

select distinct scrssn from #tempheightfinalDPP4GLP1After*/

/* Combine data from table weight and height to obtain the bmi for individual patient */

select distinct a.patientsid, a.ScrSSN, cast(a.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as HeightTime, a.VitalResultNumeric as heightresult, 
cast(b.VitalSignTakenDateTime as date) as WeightTime,b.VitalResultNumeric as weightresult, ((b.VitalResultNumeric*703)/(a.VitalResultNumeric*a.VitalResultNumeric)) as bmi

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After

from #tempheightDPP4GLP1After as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempweightDPP4GLP1After as b with (NOLOCK) on a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN
where a.VitalSignTakenDateTime=b.VitalSignTakenDateTime --a.DPP4Filltime=b.DPP4Filltime and

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After where bmi between 30 and 35

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After where bmi between 35 and 40

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After where bmi > 40

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI_After where bmi < 30

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_CreatAfter
from #tempCreatinine
where LabChemCompleteDateTime > DPP4Filltime

/*ICD9 Codes*/

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('427.31')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime
union all

/*ICD10 Codes*/

Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID

and b.DischargeDateTime >= a.DPP4Filltime

/* Drug name for DPP4 */

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose

select distinct a.*, b.LocalDrugNameWithDose, b.FillDateTime, b.DrugNameWithoutDose

into #tempdpp4glpdose

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
      LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%'  
union all

select distinct a.*, b.LocalDrugNameWithDose, b.FillDateTime, b.DrugNameWithoutDose

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
      LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%' 

select distinct *

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose 

from #tempdpp4glpdose

where DPP4Filltime = FillDateTime and DPP4Filltime >= '2015-01-01 00:00:00'

select * from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose 

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' -- 279

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%'  --703

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%'  --51

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%'  --1

select distinct scrssn 
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%' --0

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_hf_glp1_bmi where bmi>=30

select a.*, b.bmi
into #tempdrugbmi
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_Drugdose as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_BMI as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn 

select * 
from #tempdrugbmi
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' and bmi >= 30 --206

select * 
from #tempdrugbmi
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' and bmi >=30 --538

select * 
from #tempdrugbmi
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' and bmi >=30 --39

select * 
from #tempdrugbmi
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' and bmi >=30 --1

select * 
from #tempdrugbmi
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%' and bmi >=30 --0

/* Drug name for GLP1 */

drop table Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Drugdose

select distinct a.*, b.LocalDrugNameWithDose, b.DrugNameWithoutdose, b.FillDateTime

into #tempglpdose

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'
union all

select distinct a.*, b.LocalDrugNameWithDose,b.DrugNameWithoutdose, b.FillDateTime

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select distinct *

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Drugdose 

from #tempglpdose

where GLP1Filltime = FillDateTime and GLP1Filltime >= '2014-01-01 00:00:00'

select * from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Drugdose where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%'

Select dflt.UDF_ExtractNumbers(LocalDrugNameWithDose) as dose ,LocalDrugNameWithDose, DrugNameWithoutdose, FillDateTime, scrssn, patientsid, glp1filltime, fillnumber
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Drugdose

/* MI 1 week after GLP1 drug initiation using only inpatient files */

select distinct a.patientsid, a.scrssn, a.GLP1FillTime, b.DischargeDateTime as comorbidity_Dischargedatetime, c.ICD9Code as comorbidity_ICD9Code,b.AdmitDiagnosis
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_MIAfter

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('410.00','410.01','410.02','410.10','410.11','410.12','410.30','410.31','410.32','410.20','410.21','410.22',
														'410.40','410.41','410.42','410.50','410.51','410.52','410.60','410.61','410.62','410.80','410.81','410.82',
														'410.90','410.91','410.92','410.70','410.71','410.72','429.79','411.81','411.0','411.89')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >  dateadd(day, 7, a.GLP1FillTime)
union all

/*ICD10 Codes*/
select distinct a.patientsid, a.scrssn, a.GLP1FillTime, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10Code, b.AdmitDiagnosis

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('I21.01','I21.02','I21.19','I21.29','I21.3','I21.4','I21.9','I22.0','I22.1','I22.8','I22.9',
					'I23.0','I23.1','I23.2','I23.3','I23.4','I23.5','I23.6','I23.7','I23.8','I24.0','I24.1','I24.8')
and b.DischargeDateTime >  dateadd(day, 7, a.GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_MIAfter --26

/* MI 1 week after DPP4 drug initiation using only inpatient files */

select distinct a.patientsid, a.scrssn, a.DPP4Filltime, b.DischargeDateTime as comorbidity_Dischargedatetime, c.ICD9Code as comorbidity_ICD9Code,b.AdmitDiagnosis
/*ICD9 Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_MIAfter

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD9SID=c.ICD9SID 
where c.ICD9Code in ('410.00','410.01','410.02','410.10','410.11','410.12','410.30','410.31','410.32','410.20','410.21','410.22',
														'410.40','410.41','410.42','410.50','410.51','410.52','410.60','410.61','410.62','410.80','410.81','410.82',
														'410.90','410.91','410.92','410.70','410.71','410.72','429.79','411.81','411.0','411.89')
					and b.DischargeDateTime >  dateadd(day, 7, a.DPP4Filltime)
union all

/*ICD10 Codes*/
select distinct a.patientsid, a.scrssn, a.DPP4Filltime, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10Code, b.AdmitDiagnosis

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_Inpatient as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalDiagnosisICD10SID=c.ICD10SID

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_AbAfter

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatientCPTProcedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on c.CPTSID=b.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.DischargeDateTime >  dateadd(day, 7, a.DPP4Filltime)
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Surg_SurgeryPrincipalAssociatedProcedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on b.OtherProcedureCPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266') 
and b.SurgeryDateTime >  dateadd(day, 7, a.DPP4Filltime)
union all

select distinct a.*
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Surg_SurgeryProcedureDiagnosisCode as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join
Src.Cohort_CPT as c with (NOLOCK) on b.PrincipalCPTSID=c.cptSID 
where c.cptcode in ('93656','93657','93662','33254','33255','33258','33265','33266')
and b.SurgeryDateTime >  dateadd(day, 7, a.DPP4Filltime)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_AbAfter --1

/* Ablation using Procedure codes- 1 week after drug initiation */

select distinct a.*, b.ICDProcedureDateTime, c.ICD9ProcedureCode
/*ICD9Procedure Codes*/

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_AbProcAfter

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_CensusICDProcedure as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD9Procedure as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD9ProcedureSID=c.ICD9ProcedureSID 
where ICD9ProcedureCode in ('37.33','37.34','37.37','37.80','38.65')
					and b.ICDProcedureDateTime>  dateadd(day, 7, a.DPP4Filltime)
union all

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join

where ICD9ProcedureCode in ('37.33','37.34','37.37','37.80','38.65')
					and b.SurgicalProcedureDateTime >  dateadd(day, 7, a.DPP4Filltime)
union all

/* Extract patients with 1 or more refills */

select scrssn, min(GLP1FillTime) as GLP1FillTime
into #tempfilltime
from Dflt.tempglp1 group by scrssn

select scrssn, sum(FillNumber) as FillNumber 
into #tempfillnumber
from Dflt.tempglp1 group by scrssn 


select distinct b.scrssn, b.GLP1FillTime, a.FillNumber
into #tempfilldate
from #tempfillnumber as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempfilltime as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn 

select distinct scrssn from #tempfilldate --13,515

select distinct b.scrssn, b.GLP1FillTime, b.FillNumber,a.patientsid, a.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime
into #date
from Dflt.tempglp1 as a with (NOLOCK) left outer join 
#tempfilldate as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn and b.GLP1FillTime=a.glp1filltime
where b.GLP1FillTime between '2015-01-01 00:00:00' and '2021-12-31 23:59:59'
and b.GLP1FillTime >= dateadd(day, 30, a.FirstHF_Dischargedatetime)

select distinct scrssn from #date --6703

/* Remove patients that were on DPP4 1 year prior to GLP1 initiation */
select distinct a.scrssn, a.GLP1FillTime, a.PatientSID, b.FillDateTime

into #DPP4

from #date as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
      LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%' 
--and b.FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
union all

select distinct a.scrssn, a.GLP1FillTime, a.PatientSID, b.FillDateTime
from #date as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
      LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%' 

select distinct *

into #DPP4Date

from #DPP4
where FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from #DPP4 --5,395
select distinct scrssn from #DPP4Date --2,278


/* Extracting patients with 1 or more refill */

select distinct a.*--, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10ProcedureCode

into #GLP1excludeDpp4

from #date as a where a.fillnumber >=1
AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #dpp4date as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)

select distinct scrssn from #GLP1excludeDpp4--4,163

select distinct b.*

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1refill1

from  #GLP1excludeDpp4 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#date as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn 
where a.fillnumber >=1 --and b.GLP1FillTime between '2017-01-01 00:00:00' and '2021-12-31 23:59:59'

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1refill1 --4,163

/* Patients on Loop diuretics with more than 1 refill 1 year preceding to the start of GLP1 */
select distinct a.*, b.FillNumber as loopFillNumber, b.FillDateTime 

into #temploop

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1refill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempmedloop as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
and b.Fillnumber > =1

select distinct scrssn from #temploop --3,507

/*Patients on elevated BNP/PBNP 1 year preceding to the start of GLP1 */

select distinct a.*, b.LabChemCompleteDateTime, b.LabChemResultNumericValue

into #tempBNPPBNP

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1refill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_BNP_PBNP as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from #tempBNPPBNP --655

/*Patients with HF ICD codes 1 year preceding to the start of SGLT2 */

select distinct a.*, b.DischargeDateTime, b.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime--, b.comorbidity_ICD9Code as HFICDCode

into #tempHF

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1refill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from #tempHF --876

select distinct scrssn 

into #tempuniqueglp1
from #temploop
union all

select distinct scrssn
from #tempBNPPBNP
union all

select distinct scrssn
from #tempHF

select distinct scrssn from #tempuniqueglp1 --3619

/* combine all 3 to get the final cohort */


select distinct a.*--, b.FillNumber, b.FillDateTime

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Finalrefill1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1refill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempmedloop as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.FillDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
and b.Fillnumber >1
union all

select distinct a.*--, b.LabChemCompleteDateTime, b.LabChemResultNumericValue, b.LabChemTestName 

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1refill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_above50_BNP_PBNP as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.LabChemCompleteDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)
union all

select distinct a.*--, b.DischargeDateTime, b.comorbidity_ICD9Code as HFICDCode

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1refill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.Scrssn= b.Scrssn
where b.comorbidity_Dischargedatetime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and dateadd(day, 1, a.GLP1FillTime)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Finalrefill1 --2,736

'I06.1','I06.2','I06.8','I06.9','I35.1','I05.1','I05.2','I05.8','I05.9','I31.1','O90.3')
union all

select distinct a.*, b.EventDateTime, c.ICD10Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Finalrefill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Outpat_WorkloadVDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('E85.9','D86.0','D86.1','D86.2','D86.3','D86.81','D86.82','D86.83',
'D86.84','D86.85','D86.86','D86.87','D86.89','D86.9','I42.2', 'I42.1','I51.4', 'I40.9','Q23.1',
'I06.1','I06.2','I06.8','I06.9','I35.1','I05.1','I05.2','I05.8','I05.9','I31.1','O90.3')
union all

select distinct a.*, b.PatientTransferDateTime, c.ICD10Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Finalrefill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_PatientTransferDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('E85.9','D86.0','D86.1','D86.2','D86.3','D86.81','D86.82','D86.83',
'D86.84','D86.85','D86.86','D86.87','D86.89','D86.9','I42.2', 'I42.1','I51.4', 'I40.9','Q23.1',
'I06.1','I06.2','I06.8','I06.9','I35.1','I05.1','I05.2','I05.8','I05.9','I31.1','O90.3')
union all

select distinct a.*, b.SpecialtyTransferDateTime, c.ICD10Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Finalrefill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_SpecialtyTransferDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('E85.9','D86.0','D86.1','D86.2','D86.3','D86.81','D86.82','D86.83',
'D86.84','D86.85','D86.86','D86.87','D86.89','D86.9','I42.2', 'I42.1','I51.4', 'I40.9','Q23.1',
'I06.1','I06.2','I06.8','I06.9','I35.1','I05.1','I05.2','I05.8','I05.9','I31.1','O90.3')

union all

select distinct a.*, b.AdmitDateTime, c.ICD10Code

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Finalrefill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.Inpat_InpatDischargeDiagnosis as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID inner join 
CDWWork.Dim.ICD10 as c with (NOLOCK) on b.ICD10SID=c.ICD10SID 
where ICD10Code in ('E85.9','D86.0','D86.1','D86.2','D86.3','D86.81','D86.82','D86.83',
'D86.84','D86.85','D86.86','D86.87','D86.89','D86.9','I42.2', 'I42.1','I51.4', 'I40.9','Q23.1',
'I06.1','I06.2','I06.8','I06.9','I35.1','I05.1','I05.2','I05.8','I05.9','I31.1','O90.3')

select distinct scrssn from #exclusioncodesGLP1--327

/* Final GLP1 cohort after exclusions */

select distinct a.*

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11refill1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Finalrefill1 as a where a.Fillnumber >=1
AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #exclusioncodesGLP1 as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11refill1 --2,409

/* Exclude patients with EF less than 50 */
select distinct a.*, b.Low_Value, b.ValueDateTime

into #templess50

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11refill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join 
Src.VINCI_TIU_NLP_LVEF as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID 
where b.Low_Value between 10 and 49 and b.ValueDateTime between dateadd(month, -12, a.GLP1FillTime) and a.glp1filltime

select distinct a.*--, b.DischargeDateTime, c.ICD10ProcedureCode

into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final1less50refill1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final11refill1 as a where a.fillnumber >=1
AND NOT EXISTS (Select * from #templess50 as b where a.ScrSSN = b.ScrSSN)

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final1less50refill1--1,161

/* Add patientsid from cohortcrosswalk for each scrssn */

select distinct a.scrssn, a.glp1filltime, a.fillnumber, b.patientsid
into Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalRF1
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_Final1less50refill1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.CohortCrosswalk as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalRF1--1,161
select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalRF1 where fillnumber=1

select distinct a.scrssn
from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalRF1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn 


/* DPP4/SU initiation after GLP1 initiation */

Select a.* , b.FillDateTime, b.fillnumber as DPP4FillNumber

into #tempdpp4afterglp1

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
      LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%'
union all

select distinct a.* , b.filldatetime,b.fillnumber as DPP4FillNumber

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%gliptin%' or
      LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glipizide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glimepiride%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Glyburide%' or
			  LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%Tolbutamide%'

select distinct scrssn from #tempdpp4afterglp1 --831
select top 100* from #tempdpp4afterglp1

Select distinct scrssn, min(filldatetime) as DPP4Initiation_Date
into #tempdpp4afterglp1date
from #tempdpp4afterglp1 group by (scrssn)

Select distinct a.*, b. DPP4Initiation_date
into Dflt.HFpEF_dpp4afterglp1
from #tempdpp4afterglp1 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempdpp4afterglp1date as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn
where b.DPP4Initiation_Date > a.glp1filltime 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.HFpEF_dpp4afterglp1 --25
select top 100* from Dflt.HFpEF_dpp4afterglp1


/*  GLP1 initiation after DPP4/SU initiation */
select distinct scrssn from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions

Select a.* , b.FillDateTime, b.FillNumber as GLP1FillNumber

into #tempglp1afterdpp4

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'
union all

select distinct a.* , b.filldatetime, b.FillNumber as GLP1FillNumber

from Dflt.Diabetes_HF_DPP4GLP1_FinalAfterExclusions as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
Src.RxOut_RxOutpatFill_Recent as b with (NOLOCK) on a.PatientSID= b.PatientSID
where LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%semaglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%exenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%liraglutide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%lixisenatide%' 
or LocalDrugNameWithDose like '%dulaglutide%'

select distinct scrssn from #tempglp1afterdpp4 --252
select top 100* from #tempglp1afterdpp4

Select distinct scrssn, min(filldatetime) as GLP1Initiation_Date
into #tempglp1afterdpp4date
from #tempglp1afterdpp4 group by (scrssn)

select distinct scrssn from #tempglp1afterdpp4date --252

Select distinct a.*, b. GLP1Initiation_date
into Dflt.HFpEF_glp1afterdpp4
from #tempglp1afterdpp4 as a with (NOLOCK) inner join
#tempglp1afterdpp4date as b with (NOLOCK) on a.scrssn= b.scrssn
where b.GLP1Initiation_Date > a.dpp4filltime 

select distinct scrssn from Dflt.HFpEF_glp1afterdpp4 --241
select distinct * from #tempglp1afterdpp4final where GLP1Initiation_Date > dpp4filltime
